\newcommand*\pandocbounded[1]{% scales image to fit in text height/width
  \sbox\pandoc@box{#1}%
  \Gscale@div\@tempa{\textheight}{\dimexpr\ht\pandoc@box+\dp\pandoc@box\relax}%
  \Gscale@div\@tempb{\linewidth}{\wd\pandoc@box}%
  \ifdim\@tempb\p@<\@tempa\p@\let\@tempa\@tempb\fi% select the smaller of both
  \ifdim\@tempa\p@<\p@\scalebox{\@tempa}{\usebox\pandoc@box}%
  \else\usebox{\pandoc@box}%
  \fi%
}

LOAD DATA

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(here)}
\NormalTok{data\_path }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ here}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{here}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Diss\_full.csv"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{diss.full }\OtherTok{=}  \FunctionTok{read.csv}\NormalTok{(data\_path)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

LET'S START CLEANING

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#make all the columns lowercase to help with coding }
\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(diss.full) }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{tolower}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(diss.full))}
\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(diss.full) }\OtherTok{=}  \FunctionTok{iconv}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(diss.full), }\AttributeTok{from =} \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{to =} \StringTok{"UTF{-}8"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{sub =} \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{)}
\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(diss.full) }\OtherTok{=}  \FunctionTok{gsub}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"consent.*"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"consent"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(diss.full))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

Okay data is loaded. Now, we will clean it by the guidelines of the
pre-reg.

Participants will be excluded if they: 1) do not consent or 2) complete
the study in less than seven minutes or 3) exhibit no variability in
their responses (e.g., selecting all 5s or all 3s) across all Integrity
and likeability items, and 4) fail to complete the Integrity and
likeability ratings for at least one vignettes.

An attention check will be used to assess whether participants paid
attention during the experiment by requiring participants to select a
specific response to the question (i.e., ``Select the option ``somewhat
disagree'' if you are paying attention.''). We will do an exploratory
analysis to determine test if excluding those who fail the attention
impacts the data.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Initial data count}
\NormalTok{initial\_count }\OtherTok{=}  \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(diss.full)}
\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(initial\_count)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## [1] 675
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Clean recordeddate}
\NormalTok{diss.full}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{recordeddate }\OtherTok{=}  \FunctionTok{str\_squish}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(diss.full}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{recordeddate))}
\NormalTok{diss.full}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{created\_date }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{mdy\_hm}\NormalTok{(diss.full}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{recordeddate)}

\CommentTok{\# Filters by cutoff date}
\NormalTok{cutoff\_date }\OtherTok{=}  \FunctionTok{as.Date}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"2026{-}01{-}27"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{filtered\_by\_date }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ diss.full }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(created\_date) }\SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ created\_date }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}=}\NormalTok{ cutoff\_date)}

\NormalTok{count\_by\_date }\OtherTok{=}  \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(filtered\_by\_date)}
\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(count\_by\_date)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## [1] 675
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Filter by consent}
\NormalTok{filtered\_by\_consent }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ filtered\_by\_date }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(consent }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"Yes, I consent"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{count\_by\_consent }\OtherTok{=}  \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(filtered\_by\_consent)}

\CommentTok{\# Filter by duration (exclude under 420 seconds)}
\NormalTok{filtered\_by\_duration }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ filtered\_by\_consent }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(duration..in.seconds.) }\SpecialCharTok{\&}\NormalTok{ duration..in.seconds. }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}=} \DecValTok{420}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{count\_by\_duration }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(filtered\_by\_duration)}

\CommentTok{\# Exclude participants under 18}
\NormalTok{filtered\_by\_age }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ filtered\_by\_duration }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(age) }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ age }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}=} \DecValTok{18}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{count\_by\_age }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(filtered\_by\_age)}


\CommentTok{\# Exclude no{-}variability: participants who gave identical responses across}
\CommentTok{\# all Integrity and likeability items — uses apply() to avoid rowwise() issues}
\NormalTok{Integrity\_like\_cols }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"khon"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"ksin"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"keth"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"klike"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"kapp"}\NormalTok{,}
                    \StringTok{"shon"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"ssin"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"seth"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"slike"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"sapp"}\NormalTok{,}
                    \StringTok{"hhon"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"hsin"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"heth"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"hlike"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"happ"}\NormalTok{,}
                    \StringTok{"fhon"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"fsin"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"feth"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"flike"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"fapp"}\NormalTok{,}
                    \StringTok{"xhon"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"xsin"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"xeth"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"xlike"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"xapp"}\NormalTok{,}
                    \StringTok{"yhon"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"ysin"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"yeth"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"ylike"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"yapp"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{tl\_matrix }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ filtered\_by\_age }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{all\_of}\NormalTok{(Integrity\_like\_cols)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{everything}\NormalTok{(), as.numeric))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## Warning: There were 30 warnings in `mutate()`.
## The first warning was:
## i In argument: `across(everything(), as.numeric)`.
## Caused by warning:
## ! NAs introduced by coercion
## i Run `dplyr::last_dplyr_warnings()` to see the 29 remaining warnings.
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{has\_variability }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{apply}\NormalTok{(tl\_matrix, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(row) }\FunctionTok{n\_distinct}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{na.omit}\NormalTok{(row)) }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{filtered\_by\_variability }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ filtered\_by\_age[has\_variability, ]}
\NormalTok{count\_by\_variability }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(filtered\_by\_variability)}

\CommentTok{\# Exclude attention check failures (attention1 == 2 is correct response)}
\NormalTok{filtered\_by\_attention }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ filtered\_by\_variability }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(attention1 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{count\_by\_attention }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(filtered\_by\_attention)}
\CommentTok{\# Final count}
\NormalTok{final\_count }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(filtered\_by\_age)}

\CommentTok{\# Summary table}
\NormalTok{count\_summary }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{Step =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
    \StringTok{"Initial"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"After Date Filter"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"After Consent Filter"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"After Duration Filter"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"After Age Filter"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"Final"}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{Count =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    initial\_count,}
\NormalTok{    count\_by\_date,}
\NormalTok{    count\_by\_consent,}
\NormalTok{    count\_by\_duration,}
\NormalTok{    count\_by\_age,}
\NormalTok{    final\_count}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(count\_summary)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

okay now we filter by ege

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{attr\_dat }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ filtered\_by\_age }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    responseid,}
\NormalTok{    minsmart, minbetter, mincreative, minrather, minshould, minregard, mintyp, minkeep, minget,}
\NormalTok{    modsmart, modbett, modcreat, modrath, modshou, modreg, modtyp, modkeep, modget,}
\NormalTok{    majsmart, majbet, majcreat, majrath, majshould, majreg, majtyp, majkeep, majget}
\NormalTok{  )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

Pivot the dataset

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{attr\_long }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ attr\_dat }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{pivot\_longer}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{cols =} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{responseid,}
    \AttributeTok{names\_to =} \StringTok{"raw\_item"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{values\_to =} \StringTok{"choice\_raw"}
\NormalTok{  )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

find the correct columns to attribute to the variables

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{attr\_long }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ attr\_long }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{severity =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
      \FunctionTok{str\_detect}\NormalTok{(raw\_item, }\StringTok{"\^{}min"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"minor"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{str\_detect}\NormalTok{(raw\_item, }\StringTok{"\^{}mod"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"moderate"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{str\_detect}\NormalTok{(raw\_item, }\StringTok{"\^{}maj"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"major"}\NormalTok{,}
      \ConstantTok{TRUE} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA\_character\_}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{item\_stub =} \FunctionTok{str\_remove}\NormalTok{(raw\_item, }\StringTok{"\^{}(min|mod|maj)"}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{attribute =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      item\_stub }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"smart"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"smarter"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      item\_stub }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"better"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"bett"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"better\_researcher"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      item\_stub }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"creative"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"creat"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"creative"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      item\_stub }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"rather"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"rath"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"rather\_be"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      item\_stub }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"should"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"shou"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"should\_be"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      item\_stub }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"regard"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"reg"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"celebrated"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      item\_stub }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"typ"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"typical"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      item\_stub }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"keep"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"job\_security"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      item\_stub }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"get"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"job\_outcome"}\NormalTok{,}
      \ConstantTok{TRUE} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \ConstantTok{NA\_character\_}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

set up the data for the models

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{attr\_model\_dat }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ attr\_long2 }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{researcher\_ec =} \FunctionTok{if\_else}\NormalTok{(researcher }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"inspector"}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{severity\_num =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      severity }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"minor"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      severity }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"moderate"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      severity }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"major"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{responseid =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(responseid),}
    \AttributeTok{researcher =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(researcher, }\AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"investigator"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"inspector"}\NormalTok{)),}
    \AttributeTok{severity =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(severity, }\AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"minor"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"moderate"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"major"}\NormalTok{)),}
    \AttributeTok{attribute =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      attribute,}
      \AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
        \StringTok{"smarter"}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{"better\_researcher"}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{"creative"}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{"rather\_be"}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{"should\_be"}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{"celebrated"}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{"typical"}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{"job\_security"}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{"job\_outcome"}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{vignette\_id =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(severity)}
\NormalTok{  )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

Run our first model in the model build for hypo 1

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{A1 }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{glmer}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  chosen }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ researcher\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ attribute }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{    (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ responseid) }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ vignette\_id),}
  \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ attr\_model\_dat,}
  \AttributeTok{family =}\NormalTok{ binomial,}
  \AttributeTok{control =} \FunctionTok{glmerControl}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{optimizer =} \StringTok{"bobyqa"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

summarize our baseline

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# chosen = 0 (not selected), 1 (selected)}
\FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(A1)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## Generalized linear mixed model fit by maximum likelihood (Laplace
##   Approximation) [glmerMod]
##  Family: binomial  ( logit )
## Formula: chosen ~ researcher_ec + attribute + (1 | responseid) + (1 |  
##     vignette_id)
##    Data: attr_model_dat
## Control: glmerControl(optimizer = "bobyqa")
## 
##       AIC       BIC    logLik -2*log(L)  df.resid 
##   24538.9   24635.7  -12257.4   24514.9     23648 
## 
## Scaled residuals: 
##     Min      1Q  Median      3Q     Max 
## -2.0024 -0.5132 -0.3430  0.6463  7.9218 
## 
## Random effects:
##  Groups      Name        Variance Std.Dev.
##  responseid  (Intercept) 0.418855 0.64719 
##  vignette_id (Intercept) 0.002816 0.05306 
## Number of obs: 23660, groups:  responseid, 455; vignette_id, 3
## 
## Fixed effects:
##                            Estimate Std. Error z value Pr(>|z|)    
## (Intercept)                -1.20546    0.06522 -18.482  < 2e-16 ***
## researcher_ec               1.20467    0.01700  70.848  < 2e-16 ***
## attributebetter_researcher  0.53365    0.07540   7.078 1.47e-12 ***
## attributecreative          -0.14470    0.06855  -2.111   0.0348 *  
## attributerather_be          0.53692    0.06702   8.011 1.13e-15 ***
## attributeshould_be          0.64749    0.06694   9.673  < 2e-16 ***
## attributecelebrated         0.69276    0.06692  10.352  < 2e-16 ***
## attributetypical            0.46043    0.06710   6.862 6.79e-12 ***
## attributejob_security       0.77005    0.06690  11.511  < 2e-16 ***
## attributejob_outcome        0.73360    0.06691  10.965  < 2e-16 ***
## ---
## Signif. codes:  0 '***' 0.001 '**' 0.01 '*' 0.05 '.' 0.1 ' ' 1
## 
## Correlation of Fixed Effects:
##             (Intr) rsrch_ attrbtb_ attrbtcr attrbtr_ attrbts_ attrbtcl attrbtt
## researchr_c -0.131                                                            
## attrbtbttr_ -0.477  0.046                                                     
## attribtcrtv -0.516 -0.011  0.447                                              
## attrbtrthr_ -0.537  0.053  0.461    0.503                                     
## attrbtshld_ -0.539  0.066  0.462    0.504    0.520

\section{Integrity \& LIKEABILITY ANALYSIS (RQ1, RQ2) + TRUENESS
(RQ3)}\label{integrity-likeability-analysis-rq1-rq2-trueness-rq3}

Research Question 1: How is integrity influenced by role and QRP
severity? • Hypothesis 1a: Inspectors will be rated higher in integrity
than investigators. • Hypothesis 1b: The relative integrity of
inspectors versus investigators will increase as QRP severity increases.

Research Question 2: How is likeability influenced by role and QRP
severity? • Hypothesis 2a: Investigators will be rated more likable than
inspectors. • Hypothesis 2b: At low QRP severity, investigators will be
rated as more likeable than inspectors, but at high QRP severity,
inspectors will be rated as more likeable than investigators.

Research Question 3: Is the perceived trueness of the finding influenced
by QRP severity? • Hypothesis 3: Perceived trueness will decrease as QRP
severity increases.

Research Questions 1--2: Integrity and Likeability Step-up MLMs will
test Role, Severity, and Role × Severity interaction:

Model 1: Integrity/Likeabilityijk = γ000 + γ100 (Researcherj) + ν0k +
μ0jk + εijk Model 2: Integrity/Likeabilityijk = γ000 + γ010 (Severityk)
+ ν0k + μ0k + εijk Model 3: Integrity/Likeabilityijk = γ000 + γ100
(Researcherj) + γ010 (Severityk) + ν0k + μ0jk + εijk Model 4:
Integrity/Likeabilityijk = γ000 + γ100 (Researcherj) + γ010 (Severityk)
+ γ110(Researcherj × Severityk) + ν0k + μ0jk + εijk

Research Question 3: Trueness of Findings Model FT1: Truenessijk= γ000 +
γ010 (Severityk) + ν0k + μ0j + εijk

\subsection{STEP 1: Build the long-format Integrity/likeability/trueness
dataset}\label{step-1-build-the-long-format-integritylikeabilitytrueness-dataset}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Vignette metadata}
\CommentTok{\# Each vignette pair: investigator (inv) and inspector (insp)}
\CommentTok{\# Severity:minor = vignette K/S, moderate = H/F, major = X/Y}
\CommentTok{\# Integrity items: hon, sin, eth  (honest, sincere, ethical)}
\CommentTok{\# Likeability: like, app (likeable, approachable)}
\CommentTok{\# Trueness (conf): investigator only, 1 item per vignette}

\NormalTok{vignette\_map }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{vig\_letter =} \StringTok{"k"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{vig\_id =} \StringTok{"minor"}\NormalTok{,    }\AttributeTok{role =} \StringTok{"investigator"}\NormalTok{,}
       \AttributeTok{hon =} \StringTok{"khon"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{sin =} \StringTok{"ksin"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{eth =} \StringTok{"keth"}\NormalTok{,}
       \AttributeTok{like =} \StringTok{"klike"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{app =} \StringTok{"kapp"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{conf =} \StringTok{"kcon"}\NormalTok{),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{vig\_letter =} \StringTok{"s"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{vig\_id =} \StringTok{"minor"}\NormalTok{,    }\AttributeTok{role =} \StringTok{"inspector"}\NormalTok{,}
       \AttributeTok{hon =} \StringTok{"shon"}\NormalTok{,    }\AttributeTok{sin =} \StringTok{"ssin"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{eth =} \StringTok{"seth"}\NormalTok{,}
       \AttributeTok{like =} \StringTok{"slike"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{app =} \StringTok{"sapp"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{conf =} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{vig\_letter =} \StringTok{"h"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{vig\_id =} \StringTok{"moderate"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{role =} \StringTok{"investigator"}\NormalTok{,}
       \AttributeTok{hon =} \StringTok{"hhon"}\NormalTok{,    }\AttributeTok{sin =} \StringTok{"hsin"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{eth =} \StringTok{"heth"}\NormalTok{,}
       \AttributeTok{like =} \StringTok{"hlike"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{app =} \StringTok{"happ"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{conf =} \StringTok{"hcon"}\NormalTok{),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{vig\_letter =} \StringTok{"f"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{vig\_id =} \StringTok{"moderate"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{role =} \StringTok{"inspector"}\NormalTok{,}
       \AttributeTok{hon =} \StringTok{"fhon"}\NormalTok{,    }\AttributeTok{sin =} \StringTok{"fsin"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{eth =} \StringTok{"feth"}\NormalTok{,}
       \AttributeTok{like =} \StringTok{"flike"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{app =} \StringTok{"fapp"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{conf =} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{vig\_letter =} \StringTok{"x"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{vig\_id =} \StringTok{"major"}\NormalTok{,    }\AttributeTok{role =} \StringTok{"investigator"}\NormalTok{,}
       \AttributeTok{hon =} \StringTok{"xhon"}\NormalTok{,   }\AttributeTok{sin =} \StringTok{"xsin"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{eth =} \StringTok{"xeth"}\NormalTok{,}
       \AttributeTok{like =} \StringTok{"xlike"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{app =} \StringTok{"xapp"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{conf =} \StringTok{"xcon"}\NormalTok{),}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{vig\_letter =} \StringTok{"y"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{vig\_id =} \StringTok{"major"}\NormalTok{,    }\AttributeTok{role =} \StringTok{"inspector"}\NormalTok{,}
       \AttributeTok{hon =} \StringTok{"yhon"}\NormalTok{,    }\AttributeTok{sin =} \StringTok{"ysin"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{eth =} \StringTok{"yeth"}\NormalTok{,}
       \AttributeTok{like =} \StringTok{"ylike"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{app =} \StringTok{"yapp"}\NormalTok{,  }\AttributeTok{conf =} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}
\CommentTok{\# vig\_letter is the TRUE vignette identifier (6 distinct narrative vignettes:}
\CommentTok{\# k/s/h/f/x/y). vig\_id (severity) is NOT a valid vignette{-}level random effect}
\CommentTok{\# on its own — it only has 3 levels and is perfectly confounded with}
\CommentTok{\# severity\_num, the key fixed effect. vig\_letter fixes this: it captures}
\CommentTok{\# idiosyncratic vignette{-}writing variance independent of role and severity.}

\CommentTok{\#Helper: extract numeric value from labelled string}
\NormalTok{parse\_num }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(x) }\FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{str\_extract}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(x), }\StringTok{"\^{}}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}\textbackslash{}}\StringTok{d"}\NormalTok{))}

\CommentTok{\#Build one row per participant × vignette × role}
\NormalTok{tl\_rows }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{bind\_rows}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(vignette\_map, }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(v) \{}
\NormalTok{  filtered\_by\_age }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}                          \CommentTok{\# use cleaned dataset}
    \FunctionTok{transmute}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      responseid,}
      \AttributeTok{vig\_letter =}\NormalTok{ v}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{vig\_letter,}
      \AttributeTok{vig\_id   =}\NormalTok{ v}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{vig\_id,}
      \AttributeTok{role     =}\NormalTok{ v}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{role,}
      \AttributeTok{hon\_raw  =} \FunctionTok{parse\_num}\NormalTok{(.data[[v}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{hon]]),}
      \AttributeTok{sin\_raw  =} \FunctionTok{parse\_num}\NormalTok{(.data[[v}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{sin]]),}
      \AttributeTok{eth\_raw  =} \FunctionTok{parse\_num}\NormalTok{(.data[[v}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{eth]]),}
      \AttributeTok{like\_raw =} \FunctionTok{parse\_num}\NormalTok{(.data[[v}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{like]]),}
      \AttributeTok{app\_raw  =} \FunctionTok{parse\_num}\NormalTok{(.data[[v}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{app]]),}
      \AttributeTok{conf\_raw =} \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(v}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{conf)) }\FunctionTok{parse\_num}\NormalTok{(.data[[v}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{conf]]) }\ControlFlowTok{else} \ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{\}))}

\CommentTok{\#Composites \& factors}
\NormalTok{tl\_dat }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ tl\_rows }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{Integrity      =} \FunctionTok{rowMeans}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{cbind}\NormalTok{(hon\_raw, sin\_raw, eth\_raw), }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{likeability =} \FunctionTok{rowMeans}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{cbind}\NormalTok{(like\_raw, app\_raw), }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{severity   =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(vig\_id,   }\AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"minor"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"moderate"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"major"}\NormalTok{)),}
    \AttributeTok{role       =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(role,     }\AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"investigator"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"inspector"}\NormalTok{)),}
    \AttributeTok{responseid =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(responseid),}
    \AttributeTok{vignette\_id =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(vig\_letter, }\AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"k"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"s"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"h"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"f"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"x"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"y"}\NormalTok{)),}
    \CommentTok{\# effect{-}coded predictors}
    \AttributeTok{role\_ec    =} \FunctionTok{if\_else}\NormalTok{(role }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"inspector"}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{severity\_num =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      severity }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"minor"}    \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      severity }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"moderate"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}  \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      severity }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"major"}    \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}  \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  )}

\FunctionTok{glimpse}\NormalTok{(tl\_dat)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## Rows: 2,730
## Columns: 16

\subsection{STEP 3: Model-building
strategy}\label{step-3-model-building-strategy}

The analyses below use the preregistered step-up structure for each
outcome. Models are fit using maximum likelihood (REML = FALSE) so that
fixed-effect models can be compared with likelihood-ratio tests. Random
slopes are evaluated first for the fully specified interaction model and
are stepped down only when the more complex structure is singular. The
resulting random-effects structure is then held constant across Models
0--4 so that the likelihood-ratio comparisons test changes in the
fixed-effects structure rather than changes in the random-effects
structure.

\subsubsection{Model sequence for Integrity and
Likeability}\label{model-sequence-for-integrity-and-likeability}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Model 0: Null model
\item
  Model 1: Role
\item
  Model 2: QRP Severity
\item
  Model 3: Role + QRP Severity
\item
  Model 4: Role × QRP Severity
\end{itemize}

Model 1 tests the main effect of researcher role, corresponding to H1a
for Integrity and H2a for Likeability. Model 4 tests the Role × QRP
Severity interaction, corresponding to H1b and H2b.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(lme4)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(lmerTest)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(emmeans)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(knitr)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(kableExtra)}

\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Helper functions}
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}

\CommentTok{\# Extract fixed effects, including Satterthwaite df, t, and p.}
\NormalTok{get\_fixed\_effects }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(model) \{}
\NormalTok{  cf }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(model)}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{coefficients}

  \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{Parameter =} \FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(cf),}
    \AttributeTok{b =}\NormalTok{ cf[, }\StringTok{"Estimate"}\NormalTok{],}
    \AttributeTok{SE =}\NormalTok{ cf[, }\StringTok{"Std. Error"}\NormalTok{],}
    \AttributeTok{df =}\NormalTok{ cf[, }\StringTok{"df"}\NormalTok{],}
    \AttributeTok{t =}\NormalTok{ cf[, }\StringTok{"t value"}\NormalTok{],}
    \AttributeTok{p =}\NormalTok{ cf[, }\StringTok{"Pr(\textgreater{}|t|)"}\NormalTok{],}
    \AttributeTok{row.names =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{check.names =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# APA{-}friendly p{-}value formatting.}
\NormalTok{fmt\_p }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(p) \{}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(p)) }\FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{)}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (p }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ .}\DecValTok{001}\NormalTok{) }\FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\textless{} .001"}\NormalTok{)}
  \FunctionTok{formatC}\NormalTok{(p, }\AttributeTok{digits =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{format =} \StringTok{"f"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# APA{-}friendly number formatting.}
\NormalTok{fmt\_num }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(x, }\AttributeTok{digits =} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{) \{}
  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(x) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0} \SpecialCharTok{||} \FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(x)) }\FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{)}
  \FunctionTok{formatC}\NormalTok{(x, }\AttributeTok{digits =}\NormalTok{ digits, }\AttributeTok{format =} \StringTok{"f"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Rename model terms for tables.}
\NormalTok{relabel\_terms }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(x) \{}
\NormalTok{  dplyr}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{recode}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    x,}
    \StringTok{"(Intercept)"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"Intercept"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"role\_ec"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"Role"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"severity\_num"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"QRP Severity"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"role\_ec:severity\_num"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"Role × QRP Severity"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{.default =}\NormalTok{ x}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Likelihood{-}ratio comparison of two nested ML models.}
\NormalTok{compare\_models }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(previous, current) \{}
\NormalTok{  lr }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{anova}\NormalTok{(previous, current)}
  \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{Chisq =}\NormalTok{ lr}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Chisq[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{],}
    \AttributeTok{df =}\NormalTok{ lr}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Df[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{],}
    \AttributeTok{p =}\NormalTok{ lr}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Pr(\textgreater{}Chisq)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Build a model{-}fit table. Each chi{-}square is the LR test against}
\CommentTok{\# the immediately preceding model.}
\NormalTok{model\_fit\_table }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(models, names) \{}
\NormalTok{  out }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{seq\_along}\NormalTok{(models), }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(i) \{}
\NormalTok{    m }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ models[[i]]}

    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (i }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) \{}
\NormalTok{      chisq }\OtherTok{=} \ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}
\NormalTok{      df }\OtherTok{=} \ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}
\NormalTok{      p }\OtherTok{=} \ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}
\NormalTok{    \} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}
\NormalTok{      lr }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{compare\_models}\NormalTok{(models[[i }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]], m)}
\NormalTok{      chisq }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ lr}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Chisq}
\NormalTok{      df }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ lr}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{df}
\NormalTok{      p }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ lr}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p}
\NormalTok{    \}}

    \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
      \AttributeTok{Model =}\NormalTok{ names[i],}
      \AttributeTok{AIC =} \FunctionTok{AIC}\NormalTok{(m),}
      \AttributeTok{BIC =} \FunctionTok{BIC}\NormalTok{(m),}
      \AttributeTok{logLik =} \FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{logLik}\NormalTok{(m)),}
      \AttributeTok{Chisq =}\NormalTok{ chisq,}
      \AttributeTok{df =}\NormalTok{ df,}
      \AttributeTok{p =}\NormalTok{ p,}
      \AttributeTok{check.names =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  \})}

\NormalTok{  dplyr}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{bind\_rows}\NormalTok{(out) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
      \AttributeTok{AIC =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(AIC, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
      \AttributeTok{BIC =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(BIC, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
      \AttributeTok{logLik =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(logLik, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
      \AttributeTok{Chisq =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(Chisq), }\StringTok{"—"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{sprintf}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\%.2f"}\NormalTok{, Chisq)),}
      \AttributeTok{df =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(df), }\StringTok{"—"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(df)),}
      \AttributeTok{p =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(p), }\StringTok{"—"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{vapply}\NormalTok{(p, fmt\_p, }\FunctionTok{character}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)))}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Extract variance components.}
\NormalTok{get\_variance\_components }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(model) \{}
\NormalTok{  vc }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{as.data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{VarCorr}\NormalTok{(model))}
\NormalTok{  vc }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(grp, vcov, sdcor) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
      \AttributeTok{Variance =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(vcov, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
      \AttributeTok{SD =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(sdcor, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(grp, Variance, SD)}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# ICC for random{-}intercept components. Random slopes/covariances are not}
\CommentTok{\# interpreted as simple ICC components here.}
\NormalTok{get\_random\_intercept\_icc }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(model) \{}
\NormalTok{  vc }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{as.data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{VarCorr}\NormalTok{(model))}
\NormalTok{  intercepts }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ vc }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(var1) }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ var1 }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"(Intercept)"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{  residual }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ vc}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{vcov[vc}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{grp }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"Residual"}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{  total }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(intercepts}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{vcov) }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ residual}

\NormalTok{  intercepts }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{ICC =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(vcov }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ total, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(grp, ICC)}
\NormalTok{\}}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{STEP 4: Research Question 1 ---
Integrity}\label{step-4-research-question-1-integrity}

\textbf{Research Question 1: How is integrity influenced by role and QRP
severity?}

\textbf{Hypothesis 1a:} Inspectors will be rated higher in integrity
than investigators.

\textbf{Hypothesis 1b:} The relative integrity of inspectors versus
investigators will increase as QRP severity increases.

\subsubsection{Step 4a: Random-effects structure for
Integrity}\label{step-4a-random-effects-structure-for-integrity}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}

\NormalTok{    (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ responseid),}
  \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ tl\_dat,}

\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ vignette\_id),}
    \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ tl\_dat,}

\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{  T2 }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lmer}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    Integrity }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ responseid) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ vignette\_id),}
    \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ tl\_dat,}

    \AttributeTok{control =} \FunctionTok{lmerControl}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{optimizer =} \StringTok{"bobyqa"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{  T3 }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lmer}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    Integrity }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ responseid) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ vignette\_id),}
    \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ tl\_dat,}
    \AttributeTok{REML =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{control =} \FunctionTok{lmerControl}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{optimizer =} \StringTok{"bobyqa"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{  T4 }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ T4\_prereg}

\NormalTok{\} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}

\NormalTok{  random\_part\_Integrity }\OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"participant\_only"}

\NormalTok{  M0\_Integrity }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lmer}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    Integrity }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ responseid),}
    \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ tl\_dat,}
    \AttributeTok{REML =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{control =} \FunctionTok{lmerControl}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{optimizer =} \StringTok{"bobyqa"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{  T1 }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lmer}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    Integrity }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ responseid),}
    \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ tl\_dat,}
    \AttributeTok{REML =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{control =} \FunctionTok{lmerControl}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{optimizer =} \StringTok{"bobyqa"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{  T2 }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lmer}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    Integrity }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ responseid),}

    \AttributeTok{control =} \FunctionTok{lmerControl}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{optimizer =} \StringTok{"bobyqa"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{  T3 }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lmer}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    Integrity }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ responseid),}
    \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ tl\_dat,}

    \AttributeTok{control =} \FunctionTok{lmerControl}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{optimizer =} \StringTok{"bobyqa"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{  T4 }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ T4\_max3}

\subsubsection{Step 4c: Integrity fixed effects and planned role
contrasts}\label{step-4c-integrity-fixed-effects-and-planned-role-contrasts}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Fixed effects from every model, including Satterthwaite df, t, and p.}
\NormalTok{integrity\_fixed\_effects }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  integrity\_models,}
\NormalTok{  get\_fixed\_effects}
\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(integrity\_fixed\_effects) }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Model\_"}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(integrity\_fixed\_effects)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Planned comparison: inspectors versus investigators at each severity.}
\CommentTok{\# Bonferroni correction is applied across the three severity{-}specific}
\CommentTok{\# role comparisons.}
\NormalTok{emm\_Integrity }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{emmeans}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  T4,}
  \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ severity\_num,}
  \AttributeTok{at =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{severity\_num =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{emm\_Integrity\_role }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{emmeans}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  T4,}
\NormalTok{  pairwise }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ severity\_num,}
  \AttributeTok{at =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{severity\_num =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)),}
  \AttributeTok{adjust =} \StringTok{"bonferroni"}
\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(emm\_Integrity\_role}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{contrasts)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{STEP 5: Research Question 2 ---
Likeability}\label{step-5-research-question-2-likeability}

\textbf{Research Question 2: How is likeability influenced by role and
QRP severity?}

\textbf{Hypothesis 2a:} Investigators will be rated more likeable than
inspectors.

\textbf{Hypothesis 2b:} At low QRP severity, investigators will be rated
more likeable than inspectors, but at high QRP severity, inspectors will
be rated more likeable than investigators.

\subsubsection{Step 5a: Random-effects structure for
Likeability}\label{step-5a-random-effects-structure-for-likeability}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Maximal participant{-}level structure}
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\NormalTok{L4\_max1 }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lmer}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  likeability }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{    (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ responseid),}
  \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ tl\_dat,}
  \AttributeTok{REML =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{control =} \FunctionTok{lmerControl}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{optimizer =} \StringTok{"bobyqa"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{L4\_max1\_singular }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{isSingular}\NormalTok{(L4\_max1, }\AttributeTok{tol =} \FloatTok{1e{-}4}\NormalTok{)}
\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(L4\_max1\_singular)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\NormalTok{    (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ responseid),}
  \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ tl\_dat,}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Fit Models 0–4 using ONE identical random{-}effects structure.}

\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ vignette\_id),}
    \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ tl\_dat,}
    \AttributeTok{REML =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}

\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{  L2 }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lmer}\NormalTok{(}

\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ vignette\_id),}

\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{  L3 }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lmer}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    likeability }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ responseid) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ vignette\_id),}

    \AttributeTok{control =} \FunctionTok{lmerControl}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{optimizer =} \StringTok{"bobyqa"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{  L4 }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ L4\_prereg}

\NormalTok{\} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}

\NormalTok{  random\_part\_likeability }\OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"participant\_maximal"}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Planned role comparisons at each severity.}
\NormalTok{emm\_like\_role }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{emmeans}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  L4,}
\NormalTok{  pairwise }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ severity\_num,}
  \AttributeTok{at =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{severity\_num =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)),}
  \AttributeTok{adjust =} \StringTok{"bonferroni"}
\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(emm\_like\_role}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{contrasts)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{sigma =} \FunctionTok{sigma}\NormalTok{(L4),}

figure}\label{step-5d-likeability-figure}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{emm\_like\_df }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{as.data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
  \FunctionTok{emmeans}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    L4,}
    \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ severity\_num,}
    \AttributeTok{at =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{severity\_num =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{Role =} \FunctionTok{if\_else}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"Inspector"}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"Investigator"}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{Severity =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      severity\_num,}
      \AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
      \AttributeTok{labels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Minor"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Moderate"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Major"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  )}

\FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  emm\_like\_df,}

    \AttributeTok{group =}\NormalTok{ Role}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{geom\_line}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{linewidth =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}

  \FunctionTok{geom\_errorbar}\NormalTok{(}

    \AttributeTok{width =} \FloatTok{0.15}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{scale\_color\_manual}\NormalTok{(}

\NormalTok{    )}

  \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}

\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{base\_size =} \DecValTok{13}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{coord\_cartesian}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{ylim =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{))}

\subsection{STEP 6: Research Question 3 --- Perceived
Trueness}\label{step-6-research-question-3-perceived-trueness}

\textbf{Research Question 3: Is the perceived trueness of the finding
influenced by QRP severity?}

\textbf{Hypothesis 3:} Perceived trueness will decrease as QRP severity
increases.

\subsubsection{Step 6a: Trueness data and
model}\label{step-6a-trueness-data-and-model}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Trueness is only rated for investigators, with one item per vignette.}
\NormalTok{trueness\_dat }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ filtered\_by\_age }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{transmute}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    responseid,}
    \AttributeTok{minor =} \FunctionTok{parse\_num}\NormalTok{(kcon),}
    \AttributeTok{moderate =} \FunctionTok{parse\_num}\NormalTok{(hcon),}
    \AttributeTok{major =} \FunctionTok{parse\_num}\NormalTok{(xcon)}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{pivot\_longer}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{cols =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(minor, moderate, major),}
    \AttributeTok{names\_to =} \StringTok{"severity"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{values\_to =} \StringTok{"trueness"}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{severity =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      severity,}
      \AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"minor"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"moderate"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"major"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{severity\_num =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      severity }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"minor"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      severity }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"moderate"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      severity }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"major"} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \DecValTok{1}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{responseid =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(responseid),}
    \AttributeTok{trueness\_c =}\NormalTok{ trueness }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{3}
\NormalTok{  )}


\CommentTok{\# Descriptives.}
\NormalTok{trueness\_descriptives }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ trueness\_dat }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(severity) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{n =} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(trueness)),}
    \AttributeTok{M =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(trueness, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{SD =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sd}\NormalTok{(trueness, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{), }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{.groups =} \StringTok{"drop"}
\NormalTok{  )}

\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(trueness\_descriptives)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## # A tibble: 3 x 4
##   severity     n     M    SD
##   <fct>    <int> <dbl> <dbl>
## 1 minor      446  2.98  0.91
## 2 moderate   447  1.86  0.92
## 3 major      447  1.53  0.88
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# FT1: perceived trueness predicted by QRP severity.}
\NormalTok{FT1 }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lmer}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  trueness\_c }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{    (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ responseid) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{    (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ severity),}
  \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ trueness\_dat,}
  \AttributeTok{REML =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{control =} \FunctionTok{lmerControl}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{optimizer =} \StringTok{"bobyqa"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(FT1)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## Linear mixed model fit by maximum likelihood . t-tests use Satterthwaite's
##   method [lmerModLmerTest]
## Formula: trueness_c ~ severity_num + (1 | responseid) + (1 | severity)
##    Data: trueness_dat
## Control: lmerControl(optimizer = "bobyqa")
## 
##       AIC       BIC    logLik -2*log(L)  df.resid

## Fixed effects:

## Signif. codes:  0 '***' 0.001 '**' 0.01 '*' 0.05 '.' 0.1 ' ' 1
## 
## Correlation of Fixed Effects:
##             (Intr)
## severity_nm 0.000
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{isSingular}\NormalTok{(FT1)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## [1] FALSE
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Fixed effects, including Satterthwaite df, t, and p.}
\NormalTok{trueness\_fixed\_effects }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{get\_fixed\_effects}\NormalTok{(FT1)}
\FunctionTok{print}\NormalTok{(trueness\_fixed\_effects)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##      Parameter          b        SE       df         t           p
## 1  (Intercept) -0.8770718 0.1090937 3.195991 -8.039618 0.003179199
## 2 severity_num -0.7212843 0.1315047 2.999292 -5.484857 0.011928040
\end{verbatim}

\subsection{STEP 7: Robustness checks --- demographic
covariates}\label{step-7-robustness-checks-demographic-covariates}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Attach demographics to tl\_dat.}
\NormalTok{demo\_dat }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ filtered\_by\_age }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{transmute}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{responseid =}\NormalTok{ responseid,}
    \AttributeTok{gender =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{gender.identity}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{political =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      political,}
      \AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
        \StringTok{"Strongly Liberal"}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{"Liberal"}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{"Lean Liberal"}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{"Centrist"}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{"Lean Conservative"}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{"Conservative"}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{"Strongly Conservative"}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{religion =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(religion)}
\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{tl\_robust }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ tl\_dat }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{responseid =} \FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(responseid)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(demo\_dat, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{"responseid"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{responseid =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(responseid))}


\CommentTok{\# Integrity robustness model uses the exact random{-}effects structure}
\CommentTok{\# selected for the final Integrity Model 4.}
\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (Integrity\_uses\_vignette\_term) \{}

\NormalTok{  T4\_robust }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lmer}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    Integrity }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      gender }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ political }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ religion }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ responseid) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ vignette\_id),}
    \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ tl\_robust,}
    \AttributeTok{REML =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{control =} \FunctionTok{lmerControl}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{optimizer =} \StringTok{"bobyqa"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{\} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}

\NormalTok{  T4\_robust }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lmer}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    Integrity }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      gender }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ political }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ religion }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ responseid),}
    \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ tl\_robust,}
    \AttributeTok{REML =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{control =} \FunctionTok{lmerControl}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{optimizer =} \StringTok{"bobyqa"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}


\CommentTok{\# Likeability robustness model uses the exact random{-}effects structure}
\CommentTok{\# selected for the final Likeability Model 4.}
\ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (likeability\_uses\_vignette\_term) \{}

\NormalTok{  L4\_robust }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lmer}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    likeability }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      gender }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ political }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ religion }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ responseid) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ vignette\_id),}
    \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ tl\_robust,}
    \AttributeTok{REML =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{control =} \FunctionTok{lmerControl}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{optimizer =} \StringTok{"bobyqa"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{\} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}

\NormalTok{  L4\_robust }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lmer}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    likeability }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      gender }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ political }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ religion }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{      (}\DecValTok{1} \SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ role\_ec }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ severity\_num }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ responseid),}
    \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ tl\_robust,}
    \AttributeTok{REML =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{control =} \FunctionTok{lmerControl}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{optimizer =} \StringTok{"bobyqa"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# ── APA Model Comparison Tables ──────────────────────────────────────────────}

\CommentTok{\# Helper: extract fixed effects from a model into a clean data frame}
\NormalTok{fe\_table }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(model) \{}
\NormalTok{  cf }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(model)}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{coefficients}
\NormalTok{  ci }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{confint}\NormalTok{(model, }\AttributeTok{method =} \StringTok{"Wald"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  ci }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ ci[}\FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(cf), ]}

  \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{Parameter =} \FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(cf),}
    \AttributeTok{b         =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cf[, }\StringTok{"Estimate"}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{SE        =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cf[, }\StringTok{"Std. Error"}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{df        =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cf[, }\StringTok{"df"}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{CI\_low    =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(ci[, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{CI\_high   =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(ci[, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{t         =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cf[, }\StringTok{"t value"}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{p         =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      cf[, }\StringTok{"Pr(\textgreater{}|t|)"}\NormalTok{] }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ .}\DecValTok{001}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"\textless{} .001"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{formatC}\NormalTok{(cf[, }\StringTok{"Pr(\textgreater{}|t|)"}\NormalTok{], }\AttributeTok{digits =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{format =} \StringTok{"f"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}


\CommentTok{\# Helper: APA{-}friendly term labels}
\NormalTok{relabel }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(df) \{}
\NormalTok{  df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Parameter }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ dplyr}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{recode}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    df}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Parameter,}
    \StringTok{"(Intercept)"}          \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"Intercept"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"role\_ec"}              \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"Role"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"severity\_num"}         \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"QRP Severity"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"role\_ec:severity\_num"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"Role × QRP Severity"}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{  df}
\NormalTok{\}}


\CommentTok{\# Helper: model fit summary table with LR chi{-}square tests}
\CommentTok{\# Each model is compared with the preceding model}
\NormalTok{fit\_table }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(model\_list, model\_names) \{}

  \FunctionTok{do.call}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    rbind,}
    \FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{seq\_along}\NormalTok{(model\_list), }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(i) \{}

\NormalTok{      m }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ model\_list[[i]]}

      \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (i }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{) \{}

\NormalTok{        chisq\_out }\OtherTok{=} \ConstantTok{NA\_character\_}
\NormalTok{        df\_out    }\OtherTok{=} \ConstantTok{NA\_character\_}
\NormalTok{        p\_out     }\OtherTok{=} \ConstantTok{NA\_character\_}

\NormalTok{      \} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}

\NormalTok{        lr }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{anova}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{          model\_list[[i }\SpecialCharTok{{-}} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]],}
\NormalTok{          m}
\NormalTok{        )}

\NormalTok{        chisq\_out }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{formatC}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{          lr}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Chisq[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{],}
          \AttributeTok{digits =} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{format =} \StringTok{"f"}
\NormalTok{        )}

\NormalTok{        df\_out }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{          lr}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Df[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{        )}

\NormalTok{        p\_out }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{          lr}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Pr(\textgreater{}Chisq)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{] }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ .}\DecValTok{001}\NormalTok{,}
          \StringTok{"\textless{} .001"}\NormalTok{,}
          \FunctionTok{formatC}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{            lr}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\AttributeTok{Pr(\textgreater{}Chisq)}\StringTok{\textasciigrave{}}\NormalTok{[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{],}
            \AttributeTok{digits =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
            \AttributeTok{format =} \StringTok{"f"}
\NormalTok{          )}
\NormalTok{        )}
\NormalTok{      \}}

      \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{Model  =}\NormalTok{ model\_names[i],}
        \AttributeTok{AIC    =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{AIC}\NormalTok{(m), }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{BIC    =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{BIC}\NormalTok{(m), }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{logLik =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{as.numeric}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{logLik}\NormalTok{(m)), }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{Chisq  =}\NormalTok{ chisq\_out,}
        \AttributeTok{df     =}\NormalTok{ df\_out,}
        \AttributeTok{p      =}\NormalTok{ p\_out}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{    \})}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}


\CommentTok{\# ── TABLE 1: Integrity model fit ──────────────────────────────────────────────}

\FunctionTok{fit\_table}\NormalTok{(}
  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    T1,}
\NormalTok{    T2,}
\NormalTok{    T3,}
\NormalTok{    T4}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
    \StringTok{"M1: Role"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"M2: Severity"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"M3: Role + Severity"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"M4: Role × Severity"}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

  \FunctionTok{kable}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{caption =} \StringTok{"Table 1. Model Fit Indices for Step{-}Up Integrity Models"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{col.names =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
      \StringTok{"Model"}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"AIC"}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"BIC"}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"logLik"}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"$}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}\textbackslash{}}\StringTok{chi\^{}2$"}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"df"}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"p"}
\NormalTok{    ),}
    \AttributeTok{align =} \StringTok{"lrrrrrr"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{escape =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

  \FunctionTok{kable\_styling}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{full\_width =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{bootstrap\_options =} \StringTok{"condensed"}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

  \FunctionTok{footnote}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{general =}
      \StringTok{"Lower AIC/BIC indicates better model fit. Chi{-}square tests reflect likelihood ratio comparison to the preceding model."}
\NormalTok{  )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}
\CommentTok{\# APA STEP{-}UP MODEL TABLES}
\CommentTok{\# Side{-}by{-}side format}
\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}

\NormalTok{get\_fe }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(model) \{}

\NormalTok{  cf }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(model)}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{coefficients}

  \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{b  =} \FunctionTok{setNames}\NormalTok{(}
      \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cf[, }\StringTok{"Estimate"}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
      \FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(cf)}
\NormalTok{    ),}

    \AttributeTok{se =} \FunctionTok{setNames}\NormalTok{(}
      \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cf[, }\StringTok{"Std. Error"}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
      \FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(cf)}
\NormalTok{    ),}

    \AttributeTok{df =} \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\StringTok{"df"} \SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(cf)) \{}
      \FunctionTok{setNames}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cf[, }\StringTok{"df"}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
        \FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(cf)}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{    \} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}
      \ConstantTok{NULL}
\NormalTok{    \},}

    \AttributeTok{t =} \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\StringTok{"t value"} \SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(cf)) \{}
      \FunctionTok{setNames}\NormalTok{(}
        \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(cf[, }\StringTok{"t value"}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
        \FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(cf)}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{    \} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}
      \ConstantTok{NULL}
\NormalTok{    \},}

    \AttributeTok{p =} \FunctionTok{setNames}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      cf[, }\StringTok{"Pr(\textgreater{}|t|)"}\NormalTok{],}
      \FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(cf)}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}


\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}
\CommentTok{\# Format p values}
\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}

\NormalTok{fmt\_p }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(p) \{}

  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(p)) }\FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{)}

  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (p }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ .}\DecValTok{001}\NormalTok{) \{}
    \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\textless{} .001***"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  \}}

\NormalTok{  stars }\OtherTok{=}
    \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      p }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ .}\DecValTok{01}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"**"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{        p }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ .}\DecValTok{05}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"*"}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{""}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{    )}

  \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{formatC}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      p,}
      \AttributeTok{digits =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,}
      \AttributeTok{format =} \StringTok{"f"}
\NormalTok{    ),}
\NormalTok{    stars}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}


\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}
\CommentTok{\# Extract one fixed{-}effect cell}
\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}

\NormalTok{cell }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(fe, term) \{}

  \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\SpecialCharTok{!}\NormalTok{term }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(fe}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{b)) \{}
    \FunctionTok{return}\NormalTok{(}
      \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  \}}

  \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(fe}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{b[term]),}
    \FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(fe}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{se[term]),}
    \FunctionTok{fmt\_p}\NormalTok{(fe}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p[term])}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}


\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}
\CommentTok{\# Random effects}
\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}

\NormalTok{get\_re }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(model) \{}

\NormalTok{  vc }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{as.data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    lme4}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{VarCorr}\NormalTok{(model)}
\NormalTok{  )}

  \FunctionTok{setNames}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(vc}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{vcov, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
\NormalTok{    vc}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{grp}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{\}}


\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}
\CommentTok{\# ICC}
\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}

\NormalTok{get\_icc }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(model) \{}

\NormalTok{  vc }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{as.data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    lme4}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{VarCorr}\NormalTok{(model)}
\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{  total }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(vc}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{vcov)}

  \FunctionTok{setNames}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      vc}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{vcov }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ total,}
      \DecValTok{3}
\NormalTok{    ),}
\NormalTok{    vc}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{grp}

\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}
\CommentTok{\# STEP{-}UP TABLE FUNCTION}
\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}

\NormalTok{make\_stepup\_table }\OtherTok{=} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    m0,}
\NormalTok{    m1,}
\NormalTok{    m2,}
\NormalTok{    m3,}
\NormalTok{    m4,}
\NormalTok{    caption\_text,}
\NormalTok{    footnote\_text}
\NormalTok{) \{}

\NormalTok{  models }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}

\NormalTok{    m1,}
\NormalTok{    m2,}
\NormalTok{    m3,}
\NormalTok{    m4}
\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{  fe\_list }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    models,}
\NormalTok{    get\_fe}
\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{  re\_list }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    models,}
\NormalTok{    get\_re}
\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{  icc\_list }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{    models,}
\NormalTok{    get\_icc}
\NormalTok{  )}


  \CommentTok{\# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}

\NormalTok{  term\_labels }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}

\NormalTok{  )}


\NormalTok{  rows }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(}

\NormalTok{      cells }\OtherTok{=}

\NormalTok{          cbind,}

\NormalTok{            fe\_list,}

\NormalTok{          )}
\NormalTok{        )}

\NormalTok{          term\_labels[term],}

        \AttributeTok{M0\_b =}
\NormalTok{          cells[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{],}

\NormalTok{          cells[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{],}

\NormalTok{          cells[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{],}

\NormalTok{          cells[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{],}

\NormalTok{          cells[}\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{],}

\NormalTok{          cells[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{],}

        \AttributeTok{M2\_se =}
\NormalTok{          cells[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{],}

        \AttributeTok{M2\_p =}
\NormalTok{          cells[}\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{],}

        \AttributeTok{M3\_b =}
\NormalTok{          cells[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{],}

\NormalTok{          cells[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{],}

\NormalTok{          cells[}\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{],}

\NormalTok{          cells[}\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{],}

\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{    \}}
\NormalTok{  )}


\NormalTok{  fe\_df }\OtherTok{=}
    \FunctionTok{do.call}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      rbind,}
\NormalTok{      rows}
\NormalTok{    )}


  \CommentTok{\# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}
  \CommentTok{\# Random effects}

\NormalTok{  re\_grps }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}

    \StringTok{"responseid"} \OtherTok{=}

\NormalTok{  re\_rows }\OtherTok{=}

\NormalTok{              \} }\ControlFlowTok{else}\NormalTok{ \{}

\NormalTok{            \}}
\NormalTok{          )}

        \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}

\NormalTok{            re\_grps[grp],}

          \AttributeTok{M1\_p =} \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{,}

          \AttributeTok{M2\_se =} \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{,}
          \AttributeTok{M2\_p =} \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{,}

\NormalTok{  re\_df }\OtherTok{=}
    \FunctionTok{do.call}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      rbind,}
\NormalTok{      re\_rows}
\NormalTok{    )}


  \CommentTok{\# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}

\NormalTok{  icc\_rows }\OtherTok{=}
