\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}
\CommentTok{\# TABLE 10: PERCEIVED TRUENESS OF FINDING}
\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}

\CommentTok{\# Fixed effects}
\NormalTok{ft1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{summary}\NormalTok{(FT1)}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{coefficients}

\CommentTok{\# Random effects}
\NormalTok{ft1\_vc }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  lme4}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{VarCorr}\NormalTok{(FT1)}
\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Extract variance components}
\NormalTok{participant\_var }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ft1\_vc}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{vcov[}
\NormalTok{  ft1\_vc}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{grp }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"responseid"}
\NormalTok{]}

\NormalTok{vignette\_var }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ft1\_vc}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{vcov[}
\NormalTok{  ft1\_vc}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{grp }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"severity"}
\NormalTok{]}

\NormalTok{residual\_var }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ft1\_vc}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{vcov[}
\NormalTok{  ft1\_vc}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{grp }\SpecialCharTok{==} \StringTok{"Residual"}
\NormalTok{]}

\CommentTok{\# Total variance}
\NormalTok{total\_var }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
\NormalTok{  participant\_var }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{  vignette\_var }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\NormalTok{  residual\_var}

\CommentTok{\# ICCs}
\NormalTok{participant\_icc }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
\NormalTok{  participant\_var }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ total\_var}

\NormalTok{vignette\_icc }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}
\NormalTok{  vignette\_var }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ total\_var}


\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}
\CommentTok{\# Create table}
\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}

\NormalTok{trueness\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}

  \AttributeTok{Parameter =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
    \StringTok{"Intercept"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"QRP Severity"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"Participant variance"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"Vignette variance"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"Residual variance"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"ICC: Participant"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"ICC: Vignette"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"AIC"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"BIC"}
\NormalTok{  ),}

  \AttributeTok{b =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{sprintf}\NormalTok{(}
      \StringTok{"\%.2f"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      ft1[}\StringTok{"(Intercept)"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Estimate"}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{    ),}

    \FunctionTok{sprintf}\NormalTok{(}
      \StringTok{"\%.2f"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      ft1[}\StringTok{"severity\_num"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Estimate"}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{    ),}

    \FunctionTok{sprintf}\NormalTok{(}
      \StringTok{"\%.3f"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      participant\_var}
\NormalTok{    ),}

    \FunctionTok{sprintf}\NormalTok{(}
      \StringTok{"\%.3f"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      vignette\_var}
\NormalTok{    ),}

    \FunctionTok{sprintf}\NormalTok{(}
      \StringTok{"\%.3f"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      residual\_var}
\NormalTok{    ),}

    \FunctionTok{sprintf}\NormalTok{(}
      \StringTok{"\%.3f"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      participant\_icc}
\NormalTok{    ),}

    \FunctionTok{sprintf}\NormalTok{(}
      \StringTok{"\%.3f"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      vignette\_icc}
\NormalTok{    ),}

    \FunctionTok{sprintf}\NormalTok{(}
      \StringTok{"\%.1f"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{AIC}\NormalTok{(FT1)}
\NormalTok{    ),}

    \FunctionTok{sprintf}\NormalTok{(}
      \StringTok{"\%.1f"}\NormalTok{,}
      \FunctionTok{BIC}\NormalTok{(FT1)}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{  ),}

  \AttributeTok{SE =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{sprintf}\NormalTok{(}
      \StringTok{"\%.2f"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      ft1[}\StringTok{"(Intercept)"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Std. Error"}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{    ),}

    \FunctionTok{sprintf}\NormalTok{(}
      \StringTok{"\%.2f"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{      ft1[}\StringTok{"severity\_num"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Std. Error"}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{    ),}

    \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}
\NormalTok{  ),}

  \AttributeTok{p =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{fmt\_p}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      ft1[}\StringTok{"(Intercept)"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Pr(\textgreater{}|t|)"}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{    ),}

    \FunctionTok{fmt\_p}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      ft1[}\StringTok{"severity\_num"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Pr(\textgreater{}|t|)"}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{    ),}

    \StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}
\NormalTok{  ),}

  \AttributeTok{stringsAsFactors =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}
\NormalTok{)}


\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}
\CommentTok{\# Print Table 10}
\CommentTok{\# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────}

\NormalTok{trueness\_df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

  \FunctionTok{kable}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{caption =}
      \StringTok{"Table 10. MLM Results for Perceived Trueness of Finding"}\NormalTok{,}

    \AttributeTok{col.names =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
      \StringTok{"Parameter"}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"b"}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"SE"}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"p"}
\NormalTok{    ),}

    \AttributeTok{align =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
      \StringTok{"l"}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"r"}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"r"}\NormalTok{,}
      \StringTok{"r"}
\NormalTok{    ),}

    \AttributeTok{escape =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

  \FunctionTok{kable\_styling}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{full\_width =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{font\_size =} \DecValTok{11}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{bootstrap\_options =}
      \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
        \StringTok{"condensed"}\NormalTok{,}
        \StringTok{"bordered"}
\NormalTok{      )}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

  \FunctionTok{footnote}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{general =}
      \StringTok{"Trueness was centered at 3 (neutral). QRP Severity was effect coded as minor = −1, moderate = 0, and major = 1. * p \textless{} .05. ** p \textless{} .01. *** p \textless{} .001."}\NormalTok{,}

    \AttributeTok{general\_title =}
      \StringTok{"Note."}\NormalTok{,}

    \AttributeTok{footnote\_as\_chunk =}
      \ConstantTok{TRUE}
\NormalTok{  )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}
